HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B001","root_000148/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_030c2cfc3c9022b638c1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:3:2\",\"root_000148\",[\"B009\"]],[\"95:3:3\",\"root_000054\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yerleşip kalma anlamı, hemen ardından gelen insanın aşağıya taşınmasına karşı şehri sabit bir ölçü noktası yapar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000148/B001","root_000504/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0df8bf45112d9d5f46d8","text":"2. **A bounded jurisdiction awaiting judgment.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ب ل د/B001` supplies a bounded tract, and fixed `ء م ن/B001` supplies reliable security. Later `د ي ن/B006` supplies the city as a place of ordered authority, while `ح ك م/B002` supplies adjudication among people (95:8). **Structural cue:** the demonstrative “this” sharply locates the bounded place; the surah later moves through recompense (95:6), reckoning (95:7), and judges (95:8). **Assigned role:** the land defines jurisdictional bounds, and *amīn* guarantees the trust under which judgment can operate. **Abductive move:** the packet does not call the city a court; I infer a civic-legal mechanism from bounded place → ordered authority → adjudication. **Reading change:** the oath names not only geography but a protected sphere of accountable social order.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B001","root_000054/B002","root_000148/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_253f550fba05f149004a","text":"1. **The secure town as trust-place and later faith prototype.** Lexical evidence: ب ل د B001, bounded tract/place; ء م ن B001, settled security and trust; ء م ن B002 in 95:6, trusting assent/confirmation. Structural cue: the same root ء م ن appears as adjective أمين here and as verb آمنوا in the exception. Assigned role: the town's security becomes the environmental form of what believers later enact inwardly. Abductive move: I infer that the packet maps place-quality onto human response: the secure place models the trust that saves from the descent. Reading change: \"secure town\" becomes a prototype of faith as settled trust, not merely a description of civic safety.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000148/B002","root_000434/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_270a1236e79150441bb3","text":"3. **The chest/front image makes the city a body-zone.** Lexical evidence: ب ل د B002, chest and front hollow; خ ل ق B003/ق و م B011 in 95:4, well-proportioned form and stature; ح ك م B006 in 95:8, jaw/bridle restraint. Structural cue: a body-root network appears after the town: human form, stature, descent, and final restraint/judgment. Assigned role: البلد can be read as a front hollow/chest, making \"this secure place\" also a guarded bodily center. Abductive move: I infer a body-city analogy from the packet's proximity of bounded place and human form. Reading change: the secure town can read as the protected front/core of a body or community, not only an external location.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3ab45b81b08f1fd46431","text":"95:6 retroactively changed أمين from civic safety into a precursor of آمنوا: the same security/trust root moves from place-quality to human response. 95:7 then activated the shadow branches of ب ل د—confusion, dullness, collapse—because denial after the secure sequence looks like losing the very composure/security the town embodies. The city therefore became double: an external protected tract and an internal test of whether security becomes faith or collapses into bewilderment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B001","root_000054/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3d1ceeec2c0b9c989600","text":"1. **Security becoming enacted trust.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ء م ن/B001` includes safety, trust, entrusted things, and the trustworthy; the same root recurs as the verb in 95:6, where `ء م ن/B002` supplies trusting assent and confirmation. **Structural cue:** the root first qualifies a place as *amīn* and later qualifies the excepted people by what they do, *āmanū*. **Assigned role:** the city embodies settled trust externally, while the later verb makes trust an internal and communal act. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies recurrence but not mutual causation; I infer a trust loop in which a trustworthy order shelters assent and assenting people sustain trustworthy order. **Reading change:** *al-amīn* means more than danger-free: the city is trust-bearing and entrusted, and its security has an ethical human correlate.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000148/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6919ae347501ba2caf1f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:3:2\",\"root_000148\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Şehrin toz, açık arazi ve mezarlık çağrışımları, güvenli yerin insanın aşağı dönüşündeki toprak sonunu da içinde taşıdığı ihtimalini açar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000148/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7f01f529aaf37e0dec78","text":"3. **Stable residence as a foil to human displacement.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ب ل د/B009` supplies residence and staying in a place, reinforced by fixed `ء م ن/B001` settled security. Later `ر د د/B001` supplies return to another place or state and `س ف ل/B004` directed descent (95:5). **Structural cue:** a lexically stable, secure place immediately precedes the human’s best formation and subsequent downward transfer. **Assigned role:** the city is the stationary reference point against which the human trajectory becomes visible. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies stability and displacement but not their comparative function; I infer that the oath calibrates human instability against a settled civic station. **Reading change:** “this secure city” becomes an anchoring measure in the surah’s spatial logic, not merely one more object sworn by.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B001","root_000054/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8b8a33264f1e6edfd2fa","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:3:3\",\"root_000054\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Şehrin güvenilirliği, ileride inananlardan söz edilince, dışarıdaki güvenliğin insanın içinde güvene ve doğrulamaya dönüşmesinin örneği olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_912b238ef557c5958f7a","text":"95:6 supplied the decisive retrospective activation by repeating `ء م ن` as *āmanū*: security in 95:3 became enacted trust rather than a static property of land. Then *dīn* in 95:7 and doubled `ح ك م` in 95:8 exposed a second relation not obvious at first reading: the bounded trustworthy place can function as a jurisdiction whose social order culminates in adjudication.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000148/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a55fb28313cf592d6257","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:3:2\",\"root_000148\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Şehir adındaki göğüs ve ön boşluk anlamı, güvenli yeri insan bedeninin korunan merkezi gibi okumaya imkân verir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000148/B005","root_000148/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d4d05ee17854dfb98711","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:3:2\",\"root_000148\",[\"B005\",\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Şaşkınlık, çöküş ve donukluk anlamları, güvenliğin inkâr karşısında zihinsel çözülmeye dönüşebilen bir karşı yüzü bulunduğunu düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000131/B008","root_000148/B005","root_000148/B007","root_001290/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_dbc9c0cf9ed6ab59c173","text":"4. **Confusion and collapse as the town's shadow-reading.** Lexical evidence: ب ل د B005, confusion and collapse of composure; ب ل د B007, dullness; ك ذ ب B002 in 95:7, declaring false; ب ع د B008, depth of judgment. Structural cue: after the secure oath, 95:7 asks what still makes one deny after all this. Assigned role: the same root that names the town holds a branch for perplexed collapse, activated by the later denial-question. Abductive move: I infer that refusal after evidence is not mere opposition but a collapse of perception/judgment in the very place that should secure trust. Reading change: \"secure town\" carries a counterfactual: security can be answered by trust or by dull, confused denial.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B001","root_000148/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e37932cc2d13153840e7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:3:2\",\"root_000148\",[\"B001\"]],[\"95:3:3\",\"root_000054\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sınırları belirli ve güvenilir şehir, ödül, hesap ve hüküm dizisiyle, sorumluluğun işlediği korunan bir yargı alanı niteliği kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000148/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ef85ea954a26b8c11cd0","text":"2. **A bounded tract that can also be wilderness, grave, or exposed earth.** Lexical evidence: ب ل د B001 includes inhabited/uninhabited land, wilderness, cemetery/grave, dust, and exposed open space; س ف ل B001/B004 in 95:5, lowness and downward motion. Structural cue: the secure town precedes human creation and then return to the lowest. Assigned role: البلد supplies a bounded earthly field that can hold both habitation/security and grave-like/exposed endpoints. Abductive move: I supply the causal field: the same \"land\" in which humans are secured is also the earthward destination when vertical dignity fails. Reading change: the ayah's البلد is not only a safe civic site; it also quietly contains dust, exposure, and burial as latent counter-images.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
